# Riesame capitolo per capitolo — 30 settembre 2026

## Esito e limiti

Confronto registrato per **112/112 capitoli, 7/7 libri**. Conservati 994 fatti source-first. Il registro contiene 264 interventi: sono azioni di correzione, non un conteggio di difetti distinti.

**Non è una certificazione che nulla sia stato perso dal libro originale.** Il PDF master non era disponibile. Il riesame usa i sette inventari source-first preesistenti e le relative seconde letture, confronta tutti i capitoli con la produzione e ricontrolla i passi necessari alle correzioni. Non presenta queste verifiche mirate come una nuova lettura indipendente integrale delle 463 mila parole.

Il testo locale contiene una duplicazione estesa in b2c5 e alcuni artefatti di trascrizione. Occorre collazionarli con l'originale; cancellare il doppione senza il master cambierebbe arbitrariamente la fonte. I 112 testi e i sette oracoli congelati non sono stati modificati.

Gli attori senza nome, il contesto storico e i luoghi non risolti sono conservati esplicitamente nell'inventario di ricerca; non sono tutti entità visibili sulla mappa. L'accordo fra dati e dossier non è una prova di completezza.

Le correzioni sono state ricostruite il 1 ottobre dopo un ripristino del workspace precedente al commit locale non pubblicato. I risultati e i test del checkpoint ricostruito sostituiscono i conteggi del precedente checkpoint perso.

## Dati correnti

| Categoria | Record |
|---|---:|
| characters | 155 |
| events | 758 |
| states | 420 |
| groups | 39 |
| locations | 253 |
| relationships | 83 |

Il totale degli eventi include record di riferimenti a livello di capitolo. Non confrontarlo con il totale dei fatti per calcolare una percentuale di completezza.

## Riproducibilità

Eseguire `node tools/generate-source-review.mjs`; verificare senza scritture con `--check`. Il JSON conserva tutti i fatti originari, hash, roster, menzioni, stati, relazioni introdotte, interventi e decisioni sui vecchi roster.

- [Inventario completo](chapter-review-2026-09-30.json)
- [Registro degli interventi](remediation-2026-09-30.json)
- [Decisioni sui roster](review-adjudications-2026-09-30.json)

## 1. The Little Bell (b1c1)

Fonte: `data/source/book1/chapter1-the-little-bell.txt` — 293 righe, 3186 parole. SHA-256: `d6baa5287123802ec8d2499984720ff4201605b158c9c593470d42f8cd4354d9`.

**Roster fisico corrente:** akemi; matahachi; musashi; oko.

**Riferimenti negli eventi:** ogin; osugi; otsu. Presenza e riferimento possono coesistere in scene diverse dello stesso capitolo.

**Attori funzionali della fonte:** Eastern cavalry passing over the battlefield.

**Contesto storico:** Kobayakawa Hideaki; Ishida Mitsunari; Ukita; Shimazu; Konishi; Tokugawa Ieyasu; Battle of Sekigahara.

**Luoghi fisici della prima lettura:** Sekigahara battlefield; wooded hills / Mount Ibuki area; corpse-strewn field near the Mount Fuwa marsh area; Oko and Akemi's house.

**Luoghi riferiti della prima lettura:** Tarui; Mount Fuwa / marsh south of it.

**Personaggi riferiti della prima lettura:** Otsu; Takezo's sister; Munisai; Lord Shimmen.

Roster coincidenti dopo normalizzazione degli ID; non è una certificazione di esaustività.

- R054 — temporal_identity; `osugi`: Role label until the explicit name is given. (corrected; `data/source/book1/chapter1-the-little-bell.txt`).

- R058 — restored_event; `b1c1-audit-s08`: Matahachi is treated for illness and Takezo tends his bullet wounds during an extended stay. (corrected; `data/source/book1/chapter1-the-little-bell.txt`).

| b5c15-s04 | agricultural_training | Musashi lays aside the sword for sustained work with axe and hoe, treating cultivation and self-support as part of martial discipline. |
| b5c15-s05 | teacher_pupil_bond | Musashi begins teaching Iori work, reading, writing and conduct while also learning from the boy's practical weather sense and resilience. |
| b5c15-s06 | flood_failure | Repeated storms and floods destroy the fields and dikes despite enormous labor, confronting Musashi with a problem brute will cannot solve. |
| b5c15-s07 | design_reversal | Musashi realizes he has been forcing water and soil into an imported rectangular-field design rather than understanding the terrain's own nature. |
| b5c15-s08 | principle_formation | He changes strategy to work with the flow of water and contours of the land, writing that one must not oppose the way of the universe without first understanding it. |

### Fatti source-first conservati

| ID | Tipo | Fatto |
|---|---|---|
| b6c11-s01 | recognition_and_plot | Okō recognizes Musashi at Mitsumine, tells Baiken and Tōji, and pushes them to use the opportunity for revenge. |
| b6c11-s02 | identity_and_grievance | Baiken is established as Temma's brother, a former freebooter and Yaegaki chain-ball-sickle master who blames Musashi for the chain of losses following Temma's death. |
| b6c11-s03 | ambush_plan | Baiken mobilizes thirteen attackers for Musashi's inner-shrine route, including lancer priests and freebooters, with Tōji assigned a musket and Baiken his chain-ball-sickle. |
| b6c11-s04 | sunrise_experience | During the climb Iori experiences sunrise as Amaterasu, connecting the deity with the maternal bond he feels he lacks. |
| b6c11-s05 | gun_ambush | The ambush begins with a gunshot on the inner-shrine approach; Musashi orders Iori down and immediately fights the surrounding attackers. |
| b6c11-s06 | two_sword_combat | Under pressure from multiple attackers Musashi fights with long sword in the right hand and short sword in the left, applying the two-sword principle formed at the festival. |
| b6c11-s07 | baiken_duel | Baiken traps Musashi's long sword with the chain and attacks with the sickle, forcing Musashi to abandon the long weapon and solve the unfamiliar distance problem. |
| b6c11-s08 | principle_transfer | Musashi recognizes the Yaegaki chain's ball and sickle as another two-part weapon system analogous to two swords, which reveals a winning tactical principle. |
| b6c11-s09 | death | Musashi closes distance, takes Baiken's sword and kills him with a vertical karatake cut, reacting afterward with compassion rather than triumph. |
| b6c11-s10 | ally_reveal | The previously unrecognized helper is revealed as Gonnosuke, who rescued Iori and struck down the gunman and other attackers from behind. |
| b6c11-s11 | battle_toll | Afterward they count twelve dead attackers: seven killed by Gonnosuke's staff and five by Musashi's swords. |
| b6c11-s12 | enemy_escape | Okō escapes the scene while Kuro breaks loose and the surviving wounded priest moves away. |
| b6c11-s13 | voluntary_report | Musashi chooses to go down the mountain and report the killings to the local authorities because the violence occurred on shrine territory. |
| b6c11-s14 | arrest | Instead of merely taking his statement, officials bind Musashi and escort him toward town under a force that grows to roughly one hundred guards. |

| b6c12-s15 | confession | After Takuan reveals that he has just met Jōtarō's father, Jōtarō finally confesses the full secret conspiracy and his promise to Daizō. |
| b6c12-s16 | escape_instruction | Takuan gives Jōtarō money and a Daitokuji seal, orders him to reunite with his father at Shōjuan and travel disguised as priests while avoiding the Kōshū highroad and Nakasendō. |
| b6c12-s17 | treason_reframing | Takuan makes explicit that Daizō's network intends to kill the shōgun, burn Ieyasu's Suruga castle, destabilize Kantō and seize government, making Jōtarō an agent of treason rather than a mere thief. |

| b6c14-s03 | failed_weapon_recovery | Matahachi escapes the shed at night and digs beneath the locust tree to remove the promised musket, but finds only human bone because the weapon has not yet arrived. |
| b6c14-s04 | intervention | Takuan catches Matahachi at the locust tree, returns him to custody and confronts him with the permanence of his own choices rather than allowing him to erase the plot symbolically. |
| b6c14-s05 | true_identity_reveal | Takuan reveals that Daizō of Narai is really Mizoguchi Shinano, a fugitive retainer tied to the defeated anti-Tokugawa side and now wanted by the shogunate. |
| b6c14-s06 | conspiracy_interruption | Takuan explains that the musket never reached the locust tree because Jōtarō, whom Daizō had drawn into the plot, failed to arrive as planned. |
| b6c14-s07 | investigation_result | Authorities search Daizō's Kiso base after his escape and discover arms, ammunition and surviving correspondence with Toyotomi supporters. |
| b6c14-s08 | release | Musashi is released from prison after the treasure-house misunderstanding is resolved and is formally handed over to Gonnosuke. |
| b6c14-s09 | raid_and_arrest | Officials confiscate Daizō's Shibaura pawnshop and documents and arrest Akemi even though she knows nothing of the larger treason plot. |
| b6c14-s10 | political_advice | Takuan warns Hidetada that obsessively hunting every hidden opponent would consume the effort needed for constructive government; Hidetada orders light punishments and lets Takuan shape the penalties. |
| b6c14-s11 | career_request | Takuan formally asks Hidetada to look favorably on the recommendation of Musashi as a household tutor, and Hidetada indicates willingness to appoint another instructor. |
| b6c14-s12 | clemency_and_banishment | Rather than execution, Matahachi and Akemi are publicly flogged and sentenced to banishment from Edo. |
| b6c14-s13 | survival_separation | Matahachi realizes with ecstatic relief that he will live and runs off without looking back, while Akemi remains behind recovering from the punishment. |

| b7c7-s02 | self_doubt | Musashi recognizes that his obsessive pursuit of a single answer may itself be another attachment to 'leaves and branches.' |
| b7c7-s03 | circle_teaching | At the Mount Hiei pass Gudō refuses explanation and instead draws a circle around the kneeling Musashi before walking away. |
| b7c7-s04 | anger_then_insight | Musashi initially hates Gudō's apparent cruelty, then studies the circle and realizes that self and universe, one sword and two swords, are not separate. |
| b7c7-s05 | breakthrough | The circle dissolves Musashi's mental wall: he understands his previous impasse as a shadow of the confused mind rather than an external barrier. |
| b7c7-s06 | nonattachment | Musashi starts after Gudō to apologize and then stops, recognizing even that impulse as another branch rather than the root of the insight. |

| b7c9-s06 | repentance | Osugi explicitly recognizes that she misread Otsū's goodness as evil, begs forgiveness and rejects her former self-justifying interpretation of events. |
| b7c9-s07 | rescue_decision | Rather than remain in despair, Osugi decides Otsū may still be alive and calls for help so she can be treated. |
| b7c9-s08 | search_party_handoff | The Hon'iden search party finds Osugi; she immediately orders them to focus on saving the unconscious Otsū rather than on her own safety. |

**Roster fisico corrente:** akemi; akemi_baby; iori; kinami_kagashiro; kobayashi_tarozaemon; koyama_handayu; matahachi; musashi; nagaoka_sado; nuinosuke; utsumi_magobeinojo.

**Riferimenti negli eventi:** hosokawa_tadatoshi; iwama_kakubei; kojiro. Presenza e riferimento possono coesistere in scene diverse dello stesso capitolo.

**Attori funzionali della fonte:** other former Shimmen retainers; Kojirō supporter spying on the gathering.

**Contesto storico:** Annual former-Shimmen-retainer horseshoe ceremony remembers Sekigahara defeat, Hosokawa patronage and survival through menial work.

**Luoghi fisici della prima lettura:** Shimonoseki; Moji; Sado residence; Kokura beach; Itatsu Bridge/riverbank shrine area; Kobayashi Tarōzaemon house in Shimonoseki; hill overlooking Funashima.

**Luoghi riferiti della prima lettura:** Funashima; Mimasaka.

**Personaggi riferiti della prima lettura:** shimmen_munisai; kojiro; iwama_kakubei; hosokawa_tadatoshi; kobayashi_tarozaemon.

Decisione sui roster: Akemi's baby and Kobayashi Tarōzaemon are physically staged; frozen roster omissions.

Differenza normalizzata dall'oracolo congelato: solo oracolo []; solo produzione [akemi_baby, kobayashi_tarozaemon].

| ID | Tipo | Fatto |
|---|---|---|
| b7c14-s01 | schedule_confirmation | Musashi crosses from Shimonoseki to Moji to confirm acceptance of the time and place of the bout, but declines to wait for Sado. |
| b7c14-s02 | teacher_student_farewell | Musashi unexpectedly reunites with Iori, gives him serious guidance about education, restraint, life and honorable service, and accepts that the conversation may be their final farewell. |
| b7c14-s03 | native_reunion | Former Shimmen retainers who knew Munisai find Musashi and invite him to their annual private memorial gathering. |
| b7c14-s04 | political_neutrality | Musashi refuses a public evening at Sado's house because rumors already cast the duel as a factional contest between Sado and Kakubei; he does not want to compromise either man. |
| b7c14-s05 | survivor_ritual | The former Shimmen men repeat the horseshoe-making ritual that commemorates poverty after Sekigahara, gratitude to Hosokawa Sansai and loyalty to the vanished Shimmen house. |
| b7c14-s06 | deliberate_disappearance | After the gathering Musashi deliberately keeps out of public view, causing Sado and others to fear he has fled. |
| b7c14-s07 | independent_transport | When found at Tarōzaemon's house, Musashi refuses Sado's official boat because accepting it could make Sado appear to oppose Tadatoshi; he will use a private Kobayashi boat instead. |
| b7c14-s08 | iori_as_witness | Sado allows Iori to accompany him to Funashima on the condition that the boy remain composed even if they must retrieve Musashi's corpse. |
| b7c14-s09 | family_witnesses | Matahachi and Akemi learn from Nuinosuke that Musashi has not fled and take position on a hill from which they hope to watch the distant bout. |

## 111. The Marriage (b7c15)

Fonte: `data/source/book7/chapter15-the-marriage.txt` — 394 righe, 4037 parole. SHA-256: `5890dae22de2fc9eafd0a23ff162793f0fa7878fb94799f015f979bde206cc2e`.

**Roster fisico corrente:** gonnosuke; jotaro; kobayashi_tarozaemon; kojiro; musashi; nuinosuke; omitsu; osugi; otsu; otsuru; sasuke; tatsunosuke.

**Riferimenti negli eventi:** hosokawa_tadatoshi; nagaoka_sado. Presenza e riferimento possono coesistere in scene diverse dello stesso capitolo.

**Attori funzionali della fonte:** shore officials and Kojirō well-wishers; Kobayashi household/shop workers.

**Contesto storico:** Kojirō discards devotional charms to remove psychological obligations; Musashi paints immediately before departure as an expression of selfless concentration.

**Luoghi fisici della prima lettura:** Kokura shore commissioner's area; Kojirō boat toward Funashima; Tarōzaemon house in Shimonoseki; Shimonoseki beach; Heike Pine.

**Luoghi riferiti della prima lettura:** Funashima; Himeji.

**Personaggi riferiti della prima lettura:** nagaoka_sado; hosokawa_tadatoshi.

Decisione sui roster: Nuinosuke is physically staged; frozen roster omission.

Differenza normalizzata dall'oracolo congelato: solo oracolo []; solo produzione [nuinosuke].

- R251 — restored_event; `b7c15-audit-references`: Additional chapter-scoped memories, reports or biographical references; no physical presence is asserted. (corrected; `data/source/book7/chapter15-the-marriage.txt`).

### Fatti source-first conservati

| ID | Tipo | Fatto |
|---|---|---|
| b7c15-s01 | kojiro_departure | Kojirō leaves Kokura for Funashima in Tadatoshi's specially prepared boat with Tatsunosuke and the falcon Amayumi. |
| b7c15-s02 | release_falcon_and_charms | Halfway out Kojirō releases Amayumi toward the castle and discards protective charms, including his aunt's underrobe, so that only his 'naked self' remains for the fight. |
| b7c15-s03 | painting_before_battle | While others panic over the time, Musashi calmly finishes two paintings and treats the act as discipline in letting a selfless heart act without ego. |
| b7c15-s04 | private_departure | Musashi chooses Tarōzaemon's private boat and the secluded Heike Pine departure point to avoid spectacle and factional entanglement. |
| b7c15-s05 | mutual_forgiveness | Osugi apologizes for years of hatred; Musashi forgives her and asks forgiveness for the trouble he caused her, freeing both from the old vendetta. |
| b7c15-s06 | reunion | Musashi and Otsū meet face to face at the Heike Pine after years of separation; Musashi openly admits that apparent coldness did not mean lack of love. |
| b7c15-s07 | marital_recognition | Otsū asks Musashi to say that she is his wife for life; he silently assents and immediately frames the departure in terms of the duty of a samurai's wife and her husband's possible final departure. |
| b7c15-s08 | departure_blessing | After Musashi boards Sasuke's boat, Otsū composes herself, kneels on the beach and sends him away without regrets; Osugi, Gonnosuke and Jōtarō bow with her. |

## 112. The Soul of the Deep (b7c16)

Fonte: `data/source/book7/chapter16-the-soul-of-the-deep.txt` — 361 righe, 3805 parole. SHA-256: `237abe47aa4d164f830453d9a92519edf11a90b8ec93dfa92fc12da0f5355cec`.

**Roster fisico corrente:** iori; iwama_kakubei; kojiro; musashi; nagaoka_sado; sasuke; tatsunosuke.

**Riferimenti negli eventi:** akemi; gonnosuke; matahachi; osugi; otsu. Presenza e riferimento possono coesistere in scene diverse dello stesso capitolo.

**Attori funzionali della fonte:** Funashima witnesses, guards and attendants; Kojirō supporters waiting around Hikojima.

**Contesto storico:** Final duel occurs around ten o'clock despite the official eight-o'clock appointment; narrator explicitly leaves later revenge-attempt claims unresolved.

**Luoghi fisici della prima lettura:** boat from Shimonoseki through Kanmon strait; waters by Hikojima/Teshimachi inlet; Funashima shoal, reef and beach; Funashima witness stations.

**Luoghi riferiti della prima lettura:** Shimonoseki; Kokura; Moji; Hikojima; Dairinoura.

**Personaggi riferiti della prima lettura:** otsu; osugi; gonnosuke; akemi; matahachi.

Roster coincidenti dopo normalizzazione degli ID; non è una certificazione di esaustività.

### Interventi

- R043 — event; `b7c16-e01`: Corrected location. (corrected; `data/source/book7/chapter16-the-soul-of-the-deep.txt`).
- R252 — restored_event; `b7c16-audit-references`: Additional chapter-scoped memories, reports or biographical references; no physical presence is asserted. (corrected; `data/source/book7/chapter16-the-soul-of-the-deep.txt`).

### Fatti source-first conservati

| ID | Tipo | Fatto |
|---|---|---|
| b7c16-s01 | timed_approach | Musashi travels toward Funashima with the rising/turning tide and is satisfied that the late arrival will place him there around ten. |
| b7c16-s02 | wooden_sword_creation | During the crossing Musashi carves a broken oar into the wooden sword he will use against Kojirō. |
| b7c16-s03 | weapon_choice | Before landing Musashi leaves his long sword in the boat, keeps the short sword at his waist and carries only the oar sword into the duel. |
| b7c16-s04 | illegal_supporters | A group of Kojirō supporters has violated Tadatoshi's order and waits near Hikojima with lances, ready to avenge him if he loses; officials remove them from Funashima but do not fully neutralize the risk. |
| b7c16-s05 | lesson_to_iori | Sado tells Iori to watch the duel as a life lesson, not merely as a display of technique, because Musashi is putting his life on the line before his pupil. |
| b7c16-s06 | psychological_exchange | When Kojirō throws away his scabbard, Musashi says he has already lost because he has cast away his future and life, visibly shaking Kojirō. |
| b7c16-s07 | final_duel | After multiple exchanges, Kojirō's Drying Pole cuts away Musashi's headband, but Musashi's oar sword strikes and breaks Kojirō's skull. |
| b7c16-s08 | kojiro_death | Kojirō never returns to the living world. Musashi detects a faint trace of breath immediately after the blow and hopes treatment might save him, but the narrator confirms his death. |
| b7c16-s09 | respect_for_opponent | Musashi feels admiration and gratitude toward Kojirō, judging him the strongest opponent he has faced and recognizing that Kojirō forced him beyond his ordinary limits. |
| b7c16-s10 | sword_of_spirit | The narration distinguishes Kojirō's confidence in the sword of strength and skill from Musashi's trust in the sword of the spirit. |
| b7c16-s11 | departure | Musashi bows to Kojirō and the officials, returns to Sasuke's boat and leaves Funashima without administering a coup de grace. |
| b7c16-s12 | uncertain_aftermath | The narration explicitly refuses to certify whether Kojirō's supporters attempted revenge afterward and notes that later criticism of Musashi's conduct persisted. |
